aria-hidden="true"></span><h2 class="text-xl font-medium tracking-tight text-pretty text-foreground lg:text-[1.375rem]">Work in progress</h2><p class="mt-4 text-base leading-relaxed text-pretty text-foreground/70">Features and improvements we&#x27;re actively building and expect to ship soon. This list may evolve based on user needs and community input.</p><ul class="mt-6 space-y-4"><li><p class="text-base font-medium text-foreground">AI workflows</p><p class="text-sm leading-relaxed text-foreground/60 mt-1">Adding end-to-end AI workflow building blocks so you can ship chat, agents, and structured generation without assembling the stack yourself.</p></li><li><p class="text-base font-medium text-foreground">SaaS playbooks</p><p class="text-sm leading-relaxed text-foreground/60 mt-1">Curated playbooks for common SaaS paths — launching, onboarding, billing, and more — so you can follow a proven recipe instead of inventing the process.</p></li><li><p class="text-base font-medium text-foreground">Mobile app</p><p class="text-sm leading-relaxed text-foreground/60 mt-1">We&#x27;re planning to add a new mobile app starter kit, that will seamlessly integrate with the existing API of the SaaS app.</p></li><li><p class="text-sm leading-relaxed text-foreground/60">... and further improvements, fixes and dependency updates.</p></li></ul></div></article><article class="relative grid grid-cols-1 gap-8 pb-16 last:pb-0 class="rounded-full bg-primary px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-primary-foreground">Latest</span><h2 class="text-xl font-medium tracking-tight text-foreground lg:text-[1.375rem] mt-3">Marketing redesign</h2></div><div class="mt-6 space-y-4"><div><p class="text-xs font-medium tracking-wide text-primary">Improved</p><ul class="mt-1.5 list-disc space-y-2 pl-5 marker:text-foreground/30"><li class="text-sm leading-relaxed text-foreground/60">Refreshed the public site with Inter + DM Sans, olive theme tokens, a quieter Linear-style layout, dashboard wireframe hero, testimonials, and a three-bar Acme mark.</li><li class="text-sm leading-relaxed text-foreground/60"><strong class="font-semibold text-foreground">Page titles<!-- -->:<!-- --> </strong>Document titles now use `{page} – {appName}` (en dash), and every SaaS page sets a title so browser tabs never fall back to the product name alone.</li><li class="text-sm leading-relaxed text-foreground/60"><strong class="font-semibold text-foreground">UI polish<!-- -->:<!-- --> </strong>Rounded alerts and form controls, wider mail templates, smoother FAQ accordion, blog covers with tag filters, and olive touch accents across the SaaS app.</li></ul></div></div></div></article><article class="relative grid grid-cols-1 gap-8 pb-16 last:pb-0 md:grid-cols-[11rem_minmax(0,1fr)]"><div
